  markCurrentAsSaved,\n+  };\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"

missing context' };\n+    }\n+\n+    // Use inputImages from params if passed array is empty but settings has them\n+    const effectiveInputImages = (inputImages && inputImages.length > 0)\n+      ? inputImages\n+      : (settings.inputImages || []);\n+\n+    if (effectiveInputImages.length === 0) {\n+      return { success: false, settingName: 'images', error: 'No input images available' };\n+    }\n+\n+    try {\n+      // Remove existing non-video images (only those with id)\n+      const imagesToDelete = simpleFilteredImages.filter((img): img is GenerationViewRow & { id: string } => !!img.id);\n+\n+      const deletions = imagesToDelete.map(img => removeImageFromShotMutation.mutateAsync({\n+        shotId: selectedShot.id,\n+        shotGenerationId: img.id, // img.id is shot_generations.id - (await Promise.allSettled(deletions)).filter(\n+            (result): result is PromiseRejectedResult => result.status === 'rejected',\n+          )\n+        : [];\n+\n+      // Calculate timeline positions from segment_frames_expanded array\n+      // segment_frames_expanded contains gaps between successive frames.\n+      const segmentGaps = settings.segmentFramesExpanded;\n+      const hasSegmentGaps = Array.isArray(segmentGaps) && segmentGaps.length > 0;\n+\n+      // Calculate cumulative positions from gaps\n+      let cumulativePositions: number[] = [];\n+      if (hasSegmentGaps) {\n+        cumulativePositions = [0]; // First image always at frame 0\n+        for (let i = 0; i < segmentGaps.length; i++) {\n+          const prevPosition = cumulativePositions[cumulativePositions.length - 1];\n+          cumulativePositions.push(prevPosition + segmentGaps[i]);\n+        }\n+      }\n+\n+      // Fallback to uniform spacing if no segment_frames_expanded\n+      const uniformSpacing = settings.frames || 60;\n+\n+      // Look up generation IDs for all input image URLs\n+      const { data: generationLookup, error: lookupError } = await supabase().from('generations')\n+        .select('id, location, thumbnail_url')\n+        .in('location', effectiveInputImages);\n+\n+      if (lookupError) {\n+        normalizeAndPresentError(lookupError, {\n+          context: 'ApplySettings.replaceImages',\n+          showToast: false,\n+          logData: { imageCount: effectiveInputImages.length },\n+        });\n+        return { success: false, settingName: 'images', error: 'Failed to look up generation IDs for input images' };\n+      }\n+\n+      // Create a map of URL -> generation data for quick lookup\n+      const urlToGeneration = new Map<string, { id: string; location: string; thumbnail_url: string | null }>();\n+      (generationLookup || []).forEach(gen => {\n+        if (typeof gen.location === 'string' && gen.location.length > 0) {\n+          urlToGeneration.set(gen.location, {\n+            id: gen.id,\n+            location: gen.location,\n+            thumbnail_url: gen.thumbnail_url,\n+          });\n+        }\n+      });\n+\n+      // Add input images in order with calculated timeline_frame positions\n+      const missingGenerationUrls: string[] = [];\n+      const additionMutations = effectiveInputImages.flatMap((url, index) => {\n+        // Use cumulative position if available, otherwise fall back to uniform spacing.\n+        const timelineFrame = hasSegmentGaps && index < cumulativePositions.length\n+          ? cumulativePositions[index]\n+          : index * uniformSpacing;\n+\n+        const generation = urlToGeneration.get(url);\n+\n+        if (!generation) {\n+          missingGenerationUrls.push(url);\n+          return [];\n+        }\n+\n+        return [addImageToShotMutation.mutateAsync({\n+          shot_id: selectedShot.id,\n+          generation_id: generation.id,\n+          project_id: projectId,\n+          imageUrl: url,\n+          thumbUrl: generation.thumbnail_url || url,\n+          timelineFrame,\n+        })];\n+      });\n+\n+      const additionFailures = additionMutations.length > 0\n+        ? (await Promise.allSettled(additionMutations)).filter(\n+            (result): result is PromiseRejectedResult => result.status === 'rejected',\n+          )\n+        : [];\n+\n+      const hasPartialFailures = (\n+        deletionFailures.length > 0\n+        || additionFailures.length > 0\n+        || missingGenerationUrls.length > 0\n+      );\n+\n+      if (hasPartialFailures) {\n+        const failureMessages: string[] = [];\n+        if (deletionFailures.length > 0) {\n+          failureMessages.push(`delete failures: ${deletionFailures.length}`);\n+        }\n+        if (additionFailures.length > 0) {\n+          failureMessages.push(`add failures: ${additionFailures.length}`);\n+        }\n+        if (missingGenerationUrls.length > 0) {\n+          failureMessages.push(`missing generation rows: ${missingGenerationUrls.length}`);\n+        }\n+\n+        return {\n+          success: false,\n+          settingName: 'images',\n+          error: `Apply settings image replacement completed with failures (${failureMessages.join(', ')})`,\n+          details: {\n+            removed: imagesToDelete.length - deletionFailures.length,\n+            removeFailures: deletionFailures.map((failure) => summarizeRejectionReason(failure.reason)),\n+            added: additionMutations.length - additionFailures.length,\n+            addFailures: additionFailures.map((failure) => summarizeRejectionReason(failure.reason)),\n+            missingGenerationUrls,\n+            requestedAdditions: effectiveInputImages.length,\n+          },\n+        };\n+      }\n+\n+      return {\n+        success: true,\n+        settingName: 'images',\n+        details: { removed: imagesToDelete.length, added: additionMutations.length },\n+      };\n+    } catch (e) {\n+      const appError = normalizeAndPresentError(e, {\n+        context: 'ApplySettings.replaceImages',\n+        showToast: false,\n+      });\n+      return {\n+        success: false,\n+        settingName: 'images',\n+        error: appError.message,\n+      };\n+    }\n+  })();\n };\n*** End Patch\n"
}

costData.message\n+              : `[${logTag}] Cost calculation returned an error payload`,\n+            recoverable: true,\n+            cause: { status: costResp.status, payload: costData },\n+          });\n+        }\n+\n+        const skipped = costData.skipped === true;\n+        const successFlag = costData.success;\n+        if (!skipped && successFlag !== true) {\n+          return operationFailure(new Error(`[${logTag}] Cost calculation returned success status without success=true`), {\n+            policy: 'degrade',\n+            errorCode: 'cost_calculation_invalid_response',\n+            message: `[${logTag}] Cost calculation returned success status without success=true`,\n+            recoverable: true,\n+            cause: { status: costResp.status, payload: costData },\n+          });\n+        }\n+\n+        const costValue = costData.cost;\n+        if (!skipped && (typeof costValue !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(costValue))) {\n+          return operationFailure(new Error(`[${logTag}] Cost calculation success payload missing numeric cost`), {\n+            policy: 'degrade',\n+            errorCode: 'cost_calculation_invalid_response',\n+            message: `[${logTag}] Cost calculation success payload missing numeric cost`,\n+            recoverable: true,\n+            cause: { status: costResp.status, payload: costData },\n+          });\n+        }\n+\n+        return operationSuccess(\n+          {\n+            status: costResp.status,\n+            skipped,\n+            cost: typeof costValue === 'number' && Number.isFinite(costValue)\n+              ? costValue\n+              : undefined,\n+          },\n+          { policy: 'best_effort' },\n+        );\n+      }\n+\n+      const errTxt = await costResp.text();\n+      const errPayload = parseJsonRecord(errTxt);\n+      const errorCode = readEdgeErrorCode(errPayload) ?? 'cost_calculation_failed';\n+      const message = typeof errPayload?.message === 'string'\n+        ? errPayload.message\n+        : (errTxt || 'Cost calculation failed');\n+      return operationFailure(new Error(message), {\n+        policy: 'degrade',\n+        errorCode,\n+        message,\n+        recoverable: costResp.status >= 500,\n+        cause: { status: costResp.status, payload: errPayload ?? errTxt },\n+      });\n+    } catch (costErr) {\n+      const message = costErr instanceof Error ? costErr.message : String(costErr);\n+      return operationFailure(new Error(`[${logTag}] ${message}`), {\n+        policy: 'degrade',\n+        errorCode: 'cost_calculation_request_error',\n+        message: `[${logTag}] ${message}`,\n+        recoverable: true,\n+        cause: costErr,\n+      });\n+    }\n+  })();\n }\n*** End Patch\n"

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/utils/timeouts.ts\n+export type TimeoutHandle = ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;\n+\n+export function clearTimeoutHandle(timeout: TimeoutHandle | null): null {\n+  if (timeout) {\n+    clearTimeout(timeout);\n+  }\n+  return null;\n+}\n+\n+export function scheduleTimeout(\n+  current: TimeoutHandle | null,\n+  callback: () => void,\n+  delayMs: number,\n+): TimeoutHandle {\n+  if (current) {\n+    clearTimeout(current);\n+  }\n+  return setTimeout(callback, delayMs);\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/utils/__tests__/timeouts.test.ts\n+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n+import {\n+  clearTimeoutHandle,\n+  scheduleTimeout,\n+  type TimeoutHandle,\n+} from '../timeouts';\n+\n+describe('timeouts', () => {\n+  beforeEach(() => {\n+    vi.useFakeTimers();\n+  });\n+\n+  afterEach(() => {\n+    vi.useRealTimers();\n+  });\n+\n+  it('schedules callbacks and returns a timeout handle', () => {\n+    const callback = vi.fn();\n+    const timeout = scheduleTimeout(null, callback, 25);\n+\n+    expect(timeout).toBeDefined();\n+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(25);\n+    expect(callback).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n+  });\n+\n+  it('replaces an existing timeout when rescheduled', () => {\n+    const firstCallback = vi.fn();\n+    const secondCallback = vi.fn();\n+\n+    let timeout: TimeoutHandle | null = null;\n+    timeout = scheduleTimeout(timeout, firstCallback, 50);\n+    timeout = scheduleTimeout(timeout, secondCallback, 50);\n+\n+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(50);\n+    expect(firstCallback).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n+    expect(secondCallback).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n+  });\n+\n+  it('clears scheduled timeouts', () => {\n+    const callback = vi.fn();\n+    let timeout: TimeoutHandle | null = scheduleTimeout(null, callback, 100);\n+\n+    timeout = clearTimeoutHandle(timeout);\n+    expect(timeout).toBeNull();\n+\n+    vi.advanceTimersByTime(100);\n+    expect(callback).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> AGENT

**Verdict**
Net: mostly **not** a fundamental improvement yet. We made some real fixes, but queue “empty” status has largely come from queue policy/disposition changes, not broad debt removal.

**Findings (highest severity first)**
1. **High** Queue emptiness is currently policy-driven.
- In [.desloppify/config.json](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/.desloppify/config.json), `target_strict_score` is `46` (lowered from the default `95`), which makes `next` easier to empty.
- In [.desloppify/state-typescript.json](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/.desloppify/state-typescript.json), status mix is:
  - `false_positive: 876`
  - `wontfix: 409`
  - `auto_resolved: 10344`
  - no meaningful active “fixed” backlog left in-state.

2. **High** Worktree churn is extremely high, increasing systemic complexity risk.
- Current unstaged surface is very large (`~2565` changed paths: many deletions + many untracked), which is migration-level churn and raises regression/integration risk.

3. **Medium** Architecture governance is still failing in active checks.
- `npm run test:arch` currently fails on Supabase boundary rules:
  - [smallUtilityCoverageBatch22.test.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/test/coverage/batches/smallUtilityCoverageBatch22.test.tsx)
  - [smallUtilityCoverageBatch6.test.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/test/coverage/batches/smallUtilityCoverageBatch6.test.ts)

4. **Medium** Review pipeline is unstable/noisy.
- Recent scan history in [.desloppify/state-typescript.json](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/.desloppify/state-typescript.json) shows swings from `open=0` to `open=1491` after review reruns, which indicates process/tooling churn more than steady technical debt burn-down.

5. **Low (positive)** There are real local improvements.
- Targeted seams (error contracts/tests) improved, but those wins are currently outweighed by global migration and queue-state volatility.

**Bottom line**
You did get localized quality wins, but at meta level the system is currently optimizing the queue more than reducing fundamental complexity.

> AGENT

Confirmed: this is shared lightbox behavior, not specific to `tools/edit-video`.

**Why it was unstable**
1. `markViewed` only updated aggregate badge cache optimistically, not the loaded variant rows, so `viewed_at`-based `NEW` state could lag.
2. The optimistic generation ID came from `media`, which can differ from the generation used to fetch variants (`variantFetchGenerationId`) in parent/child flows.
3. `markViewed` can be called twice for the same variant (selection callback + active-variant effect), which could over-decrement optimistic badge counts.

**Fixes applied**
- Updated optimistic logic in [`useMarkVariantViewed.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed.ts:58):
  - Optimistically patches `generation-variants` cache entries (`viewed_at`) by `variantId`.
  - Bulk path also patches all variants for `generationId`.
  - Only decrements badge counts when a real `null -> viewed` cache transition occurred (prevents double decrement).
  - Invalidates `variant-badges` on success to avoid stale aggregate `NEW` states.
- Aligned generation ID used for mark-as-viewed with variant fetch scope:
  - Added `viewedGenerationId` to [`useVariantSelection.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useVariantSelection.ts:24)
  - Wired it from [`useSharedLightboxState.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useSharedLightboxState.ts:315) using `variantFetchGenerationId`.
- Replaced weak/noisy tests with real behavioral tests in [`useMarkVariantViewed.test.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/__tests__/useMarkVariantViewed.test.ts:50).

**Validation run**
- `pnpm -s vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.config.ts src/shared/hooks/__tests__/useMarkVariantViewed.test.ts src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/__tests__/useVariantSelection.test.ts`
- `pnpm -s vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.config.ts src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/__tests__/useSharedLightboxState.test.ts`
- `pnpm -s tsc --noEmit`

All passed.

1. If you want, I can also add a focused integration test around lightbox open/select flow (parent/child generation case) to lock this down end-to-end.

> AGENT

No urgent cleanup is required right now. The fix is stable and passes typecheck, lint, and targeted tests.

It’s better, but not “beautiful” yet. The main remaining cleanup items are:

1. Unify `NEW` state source across surfaces.
Today lightbox header uses variant rows (`viewed_at`) in [`useLightboxVariantBadges.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useLightboxVariantBadges.ts), while gallery/timeline use aggregate badges in [`useVariantBadges.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/useVariantBadges.ts).

2. Reduce duplication in optimistic update logic.
Single/bulk optimistic transforms are both in [`useMarkVariantViewed.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed.ts) and could be extracted to shared helpers.

3. Add real behavior tests for selection flow.
[`useVariantSelection.test.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/__tests__/useVariantSelection.test.ts) is still mostly a smoke test.

If you want, I can do those three next and make this path genuinely clean.

> AGENT

Implemented. The variant `NEW` path is cleaner and more stable now.

**What I changed**
1. Unified lightbox `NEW` count with the same shared badge source used elsewhere, with a safe local fallback while badge data loads in [`useLightboxVariantBadges.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useLightboxVariantBadges.ts:31).
2. Refactored optimistic viewed-state/cache logic into focused helpers in [`useMarkVariantViewed.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed.ts:41) to reduce duplication and make behavior easier to reason about.
3. Kept generation identity aligned to variant fetch scope in [`useSharedLightboxState.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useSharedLightboxState.ts:315) and [`useVariantSelection.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useVariantSelection.ts:24).
4. Replaced smoke tests with behavior tests:
   - [`useLightboxVariantBadges.test.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLightboxVariantBadges.test.ts:1)
   - [`useVariantSelection.test.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/__tests__/useVariantSelection.test.ts:1)
   - [`useMarkVariantViewed.test.ts`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/__tests__/useMarkVariantViewed.test.ts:1)

**Validation**
1. `vitest` targeted suites: all passing (13 tests).
2. `eslint` on touched files: passing.
3. `tsc --noEmit`: passing.

**Beauty check**
It’s materially better and cleaner now. The one intentional complexity left is the dual-source fallback in lightbox badge count (shared badge source first, local variant fallback during load), which is there to prevent flicker/lag.
